## Continuation Slice: Ordinary Teaching Route Smoke Unsafe App-Session Gate

### Summary of work completed

- Extended the ordinary teaching operations route smoke contract with unsafe app-session denial evidence.
- Added a deterministic malformed student app-session cookie probe to `scripts/teaching-operations-route-smoke.mjs`; the probe sends a teaching operation POST and audit readback with unsafe internal session claims and an invalid signature, expecting authentication denial and no write side effects.
- Added five new required route-smoke proofs/results:
  - `unsafeAppSessionPostDenied`
  - `unsafeAppSessionPostTraceHeaderReturned`
  - `unsafeAppSessionPostNoWriteSideEffects`
  - `unsafeAppSessionAuditReadbackDenied`
  - `unsafeAppSessionAuditReadbackTraceHeaderReturned`
- Tightened `scripts/production-e2e-release-gate.mjs` so stale ordinary-teaching route-smoke evidence missing the unsafe app-session results is blocked.
- Updated local-production smoke planning, route-smoke tests, production release-gate tests, enterprise static guards, and the existing acceptance packet contract to include the new unsafe app-session smoke evidence.
